} | {

} | {

};

const AUTHZ_OP_VERSION = 1;

// @public (undocumented)
export type AuthzError = {
    readonly kind: "unsupported";
    readonly message: string;
} | {
    readonly kind: "unauthorized";
} | {
    readonly kind: "unknownRole";
    readonly name: string;
} | {
    readonly kind: "invalidName";
    readonly name: string;
}
/** A define granted a feature no request is authorized against. Nothing applied. */
| {
    readonly kind: "invalidGrant";
    readonly message: string;
} | {
    readonly kind: "conflict";
    readonly currentRevision: bigint;
} | {
    readonly kind: "version";
    readonly expected: number;
    readonly got: number;
}
/** A grant refused because it reaches past one stream while the server runs
* with stream tenancy, for example a wildcard or a prefix spanning streams. */
| {
    readonly kind: "tenancyViolation";
    readonly message: string;
}
/** The mutation id already names a different request of the same account.
* Nothing applied. */
| {
    readonly kind: "mutationConflict";
    readonly message: string;
}
/** No receipt capacity is free for a new mutation id inside the replay
* window. Nothing applied. */
| {
    readonly kind: "resourceLimit";
    readonly message: string;
} | {
    readonly kind: "unrecognized";
    readonly tag: string;
    readonly value: unknown;
};

// @public
function authzErrorResultCode(error: AuthzError): ResultCode;

}

export type AuthzEventKind = {

} | {

} | {
    readonly kind: "rolesBound";
    readonly userId: number;
    readonly roles: readonly string[];
} | {
    readonly kind: "tenancySet";
    readonly tenancy: Tenancy;
};

type AuthzReply = {

} | {

} | {

} | {
    readonly kind: "role";
    readonly role?: Role;
} | {
    readonly kind: "bindings";

} | {
    readonly kind: "history";

} | {

} | {

/** Every change attributed to one stream, as Rust `AuthzSubject::Stream`. */

};

} | {
    readonly kind: "schemas";

} | {

} | {
    readonly kind: "schemaRegistered";

} | {
    readonly kind: "decoded";

} | {
    readonly kind: "unrecognized";
    readonly tag: string;

};

    readonly tag: string;
    readonly value: unknown;
};

    readonly withForeignPolicy: (filter: ConsumerFilter, foreignPolicy: RecordPolicy) => ConsumerFilter;

    readonly behindBy: bigint;
} | {
    readonly kind: "consumed";
    readonly committed: bigint;
    readonly head: bigint;
}
/** The agent's group committed past the record without handling it. */
| {
    readonly kind: "skipped";
    readonly committed: bigint;
    readonly head: bigint;
    readonly dispatch: Dispatch;
};

// @public (undocumented)
type ControlCommand = {
    readonly kind: "registerProjection";
    readonly projection: Projection;
} | {
    readonly kind: "dropProjection";
    readonly id: string;
} | {
    readonly kind: "applyBinding";
    readonly binding: ProjectionBinding;
} | {
    readonly kind: "removeBinding";
    readonly source: SourceSelector;
    readonly projectionRef?: string;
    readonly generation?: ControlStreamGeneration;
} | {
    readonly kind: "registerSchema";
    readonly schema: SchemaDef;
} | {
    readonly kind: "dropSchema";
    readonly id: number;
} | {
    readonly kind: "allocateSchema";
    readonly allocation: SchemaAllocation;
} | {
    readonly kind: "registerGraph";
    readonly projection: Projection;
} | {
    readonly kind: "dropGraph";
    readonly id: string;
} | {
    readonly kind: "registerSessionSource";
    readonly stream: string;
    readonly topics: SessionTopics;
} | {
    readonly kind: "removeSessionSource";
    readonly stream: string;
} | {
    readonly kind: "filterCatalog";
    readonly command: FilterCatalogCommand;
} | {
    readonly kind: "acceptGap";
    readonly acceptance: GapAcceptance;
} | {
    readonly kind: "skipRecord";
    readonly skip: RecordSkip;
} | {
    readonly kind: "sourceRetained";
    readonly retention: SourceRetention;
};

        readonly timeoutMs?: number;
    }): Promise<MatchedPage>;

    }): Promise<MatchedRecord>;
    owns(value: MatchedPage | MatchedRecord): boolean;

    skip(partitionId: number, offset: bigint, options?: CallOptions): Promise<void>;
    tryNextPage(options?: CallOptions): Promise<MatchedPage | undefined>;
    waitForRecords(options?: CallOptions): Promise<void>;
}

    maxWait(waitMs: number): this;

}

// @public (undocumented)
export type FilterErrorReason = "invalid_request" | "unsupported" | "version_skew" | "not_found" | "conflict" | "source_changed" | "membership_stale" | "catalog_unavailable" | "revision_disabled" | "too_large" | "unauthenticated" | "forbidden" | "unavailable" | "capacity_exhausted" | "backend"
/** A reason this build does not know. The error's `code` still classifies it. */
| "unknown";

export class FilterExecutionError extends LaserError {
    constructor(message: string, detail: FilterError | undefined, options?: {
        cause?: unknown;

}

// @public
export const FilterExpr: {
    readonly all: (children: readonly FilterExpr[]) => FilterExpr;
    readonly any: (children: readonly FilterExpr[]) => FilterExpr;
    readonly negate: (child: FilterExpr) => FilterExpr;
    readonly pred: (fieldName: string, op: CmpOp, value: FilterLiteral) => FilterExpr;
    readonly predAs: (fieldName: string, op: CmpOp, value: FilterLiteral, coerce: Coerce) => FilterExpr;
    readonly present: (path: string) => FilterExpr;
    readonly absent: (path: string) => FilterExpr;
    readonly tryPresent: (path: string) => FilterExpr | undefined;
    readonly tryAbsent: (path: string) => FilterExpr | undefined;
    readonly header: (key: string, op: CmpOp, value: FilterLiteral) => FilterExpr;
    readonly text: (fieldName: string, kind: TextMatch, pattern: string, caseInsensitive?: boolean) => FilterExpr;
    readonly headerText: (key: string, kind: TextMatch, pattern: string, caseInsensitive?: boolean) => FilterExpr;
};

// @public
export type FilterExpr = {
    readonly kind: "all";
    readonly children: readonly FilterExpr[];
} | {
    readonly kind: "any";
    readonly children: readonly FilterExpr[];
} | {
    readonly kind: "not";
    readonly child: FilterExpr;
} | {
    readonly kind: "pred";
    readonly predicate: Predicate;
} | {
    readonly kind: "pred_as";
    readonly predicate: CoercedPredicate;
} | {
    readonly kind: "present";
    readonly path: FieldPath;
} | {
    readonly kind: "absent";
    readonly path: FieldPath;
} | {
    readonly kind: "header";
    readonly predicate: HeaderPredicate;
} | {
    readonly kind: "text";
    readonly predicate: TextPredicate;
} | {
    readonly kind: "header_text";
    readonly predicate: TextPredicate;
};

// @public (undocumented)
type FilterMutation = {
    readonly kind: "set_revision_enabled";

} | {
    readonly kind: "register";
    readonly name: string;
    readonly description: string;
    readonly filter: ConsumerFilter;
} | {
    readonly kind: "revise";
    readonly filterId: number;
    readonly expectedRevision: number;
    readonly filter: ConsumerFilter;
} | {
    readonly kind: "describe";
    readonly filterId: number;
    readonly description: string;
} | {
    readonly kind: "archive";
    readonly filterId: number;
} | {
    readonly kind: "drop";
    readonly filterId: number;
} | {
    readonly kind: "bind";
    readonly group: FilterGroupRef;
    readonly filterId: number;
    readonly revision: number;
} | {
    readonly kind: "unbind";
    readonly group: FilterGroupRef;
    readonly expectedDigest: Uint8Array;
    readonly expectedIdentity?: FilterGroupIdentity;
} | {
    readonly kind: "configure_group";

    readonly policy: GroupFilterSpec;
    readonly expectedIdentity?: FilterGroupIdentity;
};

}

type FilterMutationResult = {

} | {
    readonly kind: "registered";

} | {
    readonly kind: "revised";

} | {
    readonly kind: "described";
    readonly filterId: number;
} | {
    readonly kind: "archived";
    readonly filterId: number;
} | {
    readonly kind: "dropped";
    readonly filterId: number;
} | {
    readonly kind: "bound";

} | {
    readonly kind: "unbound";

};

};

    inSession(session: SessionRef): Kv;
    lease(key: Uint8Array, holderId: string, ttlMs: number, options?: CallOptions): Promise<Lease>;
    leaseGuard(key: Uint8Array, holderId: string, ttlMs: number, options?: CallOptions): Promise<LeaseGuard>;

    get namespace(): string;

    patchIfVersion(key: Uint8Array, patch: Uint8Array, version: bigint, options?: CallOptions): Promise<bigint>;
    release(key: Uint8Array, holderId: string, token: bigint, options?: CallOptions): Promise<boolean>;

export type KvError = {
    readonly kind: "unsupported";

    readonly kind: "invalidNamespace";

} | {
    readonly kind: "tooLarge";

} | {
    readonly kind: "backend";

} | {
    readonly kind: "unavailable";
    readonly message: string;
} | {
    readonly kind: "version";
    readonly expected: number;
    readonly got: number;
} | {
    readonly kind: "versionConflict";
    readonly current?: bigint;
} | {
    readonly kind: "leaseLost";
} | {
    readonly kind: "leaseHeld";
    readonly expiresAtMicros?: bigint;
} | {
    readonly kind: "notFound";
} | {
    readonly kind: "notLeader";
} | {
    readonly kind: "stale";
    readonly required: MutationPosition;
} | {
    readonly kind: "barrierUnreachable";
    readonly required: MutationPosition;
} | {
    readonly kind: "forbidden";
    readonly message: string;
} | {
    readonly kind: "invalidExpiry";
    readonly message: string;
} | {
    readonly kind: "unrecognized";
    readonly tag: string;
    readonly value: unknown;
};

// @public (undocumented)
type KvOutcome = {
    readonly kind: "value";
    readonly entry?: KvEntry;
} | {
    readonly kind: "written";
} | {
    readonly kind: "committed";
    readonly version: bigint;
} | {
    readonly kind: "deleted";
    readonly removed: boolean;
} | {
    readonly kind: "deletedMany";
    readonly count: number;
} | {
    readonly kind: "page";
    readonly page: KvPage;
} | {
    readonly kind: "namespaces";
    readonly namespaces: readonly KvNamespaceInfo[];
} | {
    readonly kind: "notModified";
} | {
    readonly kind: "metadata";
    readonly metadata?: KvMetadata;
} | {
    readonly kind: "versioned";
    readonly version: bigint;
} | {
    readonly kind: "leased";
    readonly leaseToken: bigint;
    readonly grantedTtlMicros: bigint;
    readonly position: MutationPosition;
} | {
    readonly kind: "renewed";
    readonly leaseToken: bigint;
    readonly grantedTtlMicros: bigint;
    readonly position: MutationPosition;
} | {
    readonly kind: "released";
    readonly wasHeld: boolean;
} | {
    readonly kind: "unrecognized";
    readonly tag: string;
    readonly value: unknown;
};

// @public
export type SessionLayout =
/** One `agent.sessions` topic keyed by session. The default. */
    {
    readonly kind: "shared";
}
/** Each declared agent reads its own topic, keyed by session. Commands,
* replies, and chunks addressed to a declared agent on `agent.sessions`
* land on its topic, everything else stays on the lane. */
| {
    readonly kind: "perAgentTopic";
    readonly topics: ReadonlyMap<string, string>;
}
/** Work rides `agent.sessions` on a declared partition per agent. */
| {
    readonly kind: "perAgentPartition";
    readonly partitions: ReadonlyMap<string, number>;
}
/** Everything on one partition. */
| {
    readonly kind: "singlePartition";
};

export interface WireAgentCard {

    // (undocumented)
    readonly version?: string;
}

// @public (undocumented)
export type WireAgentId = string & {
    readonly __brand: "AgentId";
};

// @public (undocumented)
export class WireConversationId extends WireId<"ConversationId"> {
    // (undocumented)
    static fromBytes(bytes: Uint8Array): WireConversationId;
    // (undocumented)
    static fromU128(value: bigint): WireConversationId;
    // (undocumented)
    static parse(text: string): WireConversationId;
    // (undocumented)
    static tryParse(text: string): WireConversationId | undefined;
}

// @public (undocumented)
export abstract class WireId<Brand extends string> {
    protected constructor(value: bigint);
    // (undocumented)
    asU128(): bigint;
    // (undocumented)
    protected readonly brand: Brand;
    // (undocumented)
    equals(other: WireId<Brand>): boolean;
    // (undocumented)
    toBytes(): Uint8Array;
    // (undocumented)
    toString(): string;
}

// @public (undocumented)
export type WireSessionError = {
    readonly kind: SessionErrorKind;
    readonly message: string;
} | {
    readonly kind: "unrecognized";
    readonly tag: string;

};

}
